## Shared findings from the last pass and their disposition (all measured by the Opus 5.5 seat, then closed)

Last pass (repair 3, 40 rows): codex + grok predicted, Opus MEASURED: 14 of 20 fix-side mutants kept 40/40 --
(a) helper projection widened to undriven bits (`0x3f`, `|ARM_FLAG_C`): rows only ever set upper bits 0xc00000c0;
(b) decline flag provenance (N/Z kept from old CPSR, 7 forms): old CPSR N/Z already equalled the SPSR's;
(c) the S-instance cells of the enclosing predicates (DPI rd==PC under S; pop `return_flag || s_bit`);
(e)-graded: DPI repair compiled into MOV only; msr `~0x0f` at non-SVC; `mask &= ~0x3f`.  agy + minimax SHIP and
deepseek's 0-breach answer were therefore wrong; codex + grok held.  ALL ADOPTED.

**THE CLOSURE RULE now applied (Opus's): on each path (msr, DPI, pop) every operand bit is SET in at least one
accept; at every decline every restored bit DIFFERS between the old CPSR and the SPSR.**  Rows: src accepts SPSR
0xffffffdf, `msr cpsr_fsx,#0` + label re-entry before each decline (declines read `ffffffc0|mode`); `tbit` (T-set
accept 0x3f / decline 0x34 on all three paths); `encl2` (`adds r0,pc,#0`, `ldmia r5,{r0}^`, `subs pc,lr,#0`
declining 0x14, `msr cpsr_c,#0x03` from FIQ); `msr wide` (`msr cpsr_fsxc,r0` with r0=0xffffffd1, then a 0x14
decline from FIQ, field-only).  Six false contract/PRETABLE claims corrected.

## Probe header: contract, NOT CLAIMED, tally (verbatim)

```
CONTRACT (floor clause (e)) -- the edits this detector is DESIGNED to catch:
  * exit(1) reinstated at Save's default: (dbg save, dbg both, latch rpt) or Load's (dbg both,
    latch rpt);
  * the helper answering 1 always (kill 14/15/1e), admitting 0x1c-0x1e (kill 1e), dropping its
    `& ARM_FLAG_MODE' projection (src rows), or WIDENING it by ANY bit 31:5 (closure rule (1):
    src rows, tbit, msr wide, msr lbl);
  * the guard dropped at msr_imm (kill rows), arm_pop (rst ldm, src pop x FIQ IRQ ABT UND SVC) or
    DPI (rst movs, zero spsr, src dpi x FIQ IRQ ABT UND SVC), or restricted to ONE label, or to
    one unmodelled value such as 0x14 (src rows decline 0x0f), or compiled into the MOV instance
    only (encl2: `subs pc,lr');
  * the msr_imm guard skipped at one current-mode label (msr lbl: a decline from each of FIQ IRQ
    ABT UND SYS; latch rpt: SVC with the latch set);
  * the msr_imm decline clearing fewer than all five mode bits, at any label (`& ~0x0f': kill 03
    at SVC, encl2 at FIQ), or clearing MORE (`& ~0x3f', T lost: tbit); the restore keep-mode mask
    clearing fewer than bits 0-3 (src rows, per the bit/label table above);
  * a restore guard made unconditional, or unconditional for one label, or skipped / forced for one
    label x latch-state cell (src rows: all 40 cells; acc movs, acc ldm);
  * a WHOLE-OPERATION decline in msr_imm instead of field-only (the `if=' pin on kill rows; msr
    wide);
  * a decline taking ANY bit 31:5 from the old CPSR (or forcing it) instead of the SPSR / operand
    (closure rule (2): src rows R2/R3, tbit, msr wide) -- this replaces the repair-3 sentence
    "the declined SPSR has N Z I F SET while the CPSR it replaces has them CLEAR", which was FALSE
    for N and Z (the replaced CPSR then was c00000xx);
  * any two of the five latch sites sharing a bit (latch rpt: all five in one process; also
    dbg both, latch ind);
  * a latch that never SUPPRESSES (`|=' -> `&=', or the `|=' line deleted), or a repair applied
    only while its latch is clear (latch rpt: every site visited TWICE; src rows per label);
  * the enclosing predicates widened in ANY of their cells (see the closure paragraph): arm_pop's
    `if (return_flag)' (encl: non-S LDM with pc; encl2: S-LDM without pc), the DPI template's
    `rd == PC' compare (encl: non-S `add r0,pc'; encl2: S `adds r0,pc'), and the restore's
    `#ifdef A__S' (encl: non-S `add pc,r6,#0' -- NOT `mov pc,lr', which the decoder turns into
    `ret' and so never reaches the DPI template).
NOT CLAIMED, disclosed: unmodelled values a helper could get wrong unseen -- those never driven
through ANY path: 0x01, 0x02, 0x04-0x0e, 0x16, 0x18-0x1a, 0x1c, 0x1d.  Per path: msr_imm drives
0x03 0x14 0x15 0x1e (and 0x14 with T set); DPI drives 0x00 (SVC only), 0x0f, 0x14 (SVC, and 0x34
in tbit); arm_pop drives 0x0f, 0x14 (SVC, and 0x34 in tbit).  ACCEPT-side provenance of the
restored bits: at R1/R4 the replaced CPSR and the SPSR agree on I/F, so a restore that kept I/F
from the old CPSR on an ACCEPT only would go unseen -- that is the pre-existing `cpsr = spsr'
assignment, outside the repair path (graded (e)), and any unconditional form is caught at R2.
DPI opcode instances other than MOV and SUB (graded (e): the opcode flows into nothing the repair
reads).  The msr_imm comment's "only the LOCAL mask changes: ic->arg[] is a cached translation"
-- no row re-executes ONE translated MSR, so a guard that wrote ic->arg[] would go unseen;
per-CPU latch isolation (one CPU only); exception entry as a Save-backstop route (no row);
T-bit / execution-state coherence on restore; the USR/SYS `LDM {pc}^' and `MSR SPSR' paths that
stop the CPU (filed separately).  The `tbit' row reads CPSR with the debugger's `print cpsr'
(low byte only: the raw word's top nibble can lag `flags') -- a rung-3 debugger observation.

NAMED-RED tally: HEAD = 17/43, RED exactly the 26 rows kill 14, kill 15, save 2s, kill 1e,
kill 03, rst movs, rst ldm, zero spsr, dbg save, dbg both, latch ind, latch rpt, the ten src *
rows, msr lbl, tbit, encl2 and msr wide, CONTROL=OK.  `encl' drives no unmodelled mode and is
GREEN on HEAD.  `save 2s' on HEAD is the SAME Load death as `kill 14' (EOF on the first MSR) -- it
is not an independent HEAD reproduction of Save; nor is `dbg both' a HEAD reproduction of Load
(HEAD dies in Save first).  43/43 on HEAD is a broken vehicle, not success.

Stay on `step', never `continue' (IRQ dispatch is inside `if (!single_step)').
```

```diff
 /*
+ *  arm_mode_is_modelled():
+ *
+ *  #562: 1 if M[4:0] of `mode' is one of the seven modes this model banks
+ *  (10 11 12 13 17 1b 1f), else 0.  Its case list MUST stay equal to the
+ *  case lists of arm_save_register_bank() and arm_load_register_bank()
+ *  below: the three switches together define "modelled", and those two
+ *  default: arms are reached only for a mode this returns 0 for.  Extern,
+ *  not static: the DPI template is compiled as its own object.
+ */
+int arm_mode_is_modelled(uint32_t mode)
+{
+	switch (mode & ARM_FLAG_MODE) {
+	case ARM_MODE_USR32:
+	case ARM_MODE_SYS32:
+	case ARM_MODE_FIQ32:
+	case ARM_MODE_IRQ32:
+	case ARM_MODE_SVC32:
+	case ARM_MODE_ABT32:
+	case ARM_MODE_UND32:
+		return 1;
+	default:return 0;
+	}
+}
+

+		    after Load for an odd destination PC.  Only when an SPSR was
+		    copied; no early return (pc is set).  */
+		if (spsr_copied && !arm_mode_is_modelled(new_cpsr)) {
+			if (!(cpu->cd.arm.mode_warned & ARM_MODEWARN_DPI)) {
+				cpu->cd.arm.mode_warned |= ARM_MODEWARN_DPI;
+				fatal("[ dpi S-to-pc: SPSR mode 0x%02x is not "
+				    "modelled; mode kept (once) ]\n",
+				    (int)(new_cpsr & ARM_FLAG_MODE));
+			}
+			new_cpsr = (new_cpsr & ~ARM_FLAG_MODE) | cur_mode;
+		}
+		cpu->cd.arm.cpsr = new_cpsr;
 		cpu->cd.arm.flags = cpu->cd.arm.cpsr >> 28;
 		arm_load_register_bank(cpu);
 #else

```

## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)

**Do not run `run.sh`, any `gate_*.sh`, or the battery.** Every measurement you need is
inlined above. A gate takes minutes -- longer than your response budget -- and a second
concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone
else s. Building and running a single detector or mutant in your own temp directory is
fine and encouraged; driving the harness is not.

If you want a measurement that is not inlined, NAME IT and say why. Naming it is worth
far more to this panel than a timeout.

The floor it adds is {labels} x the guard operand's body-tested partition -- six cells there, two rows owed -- never the labels' cross-product with everything.**  **AND A GUARD THAT SUPPRESSES A DISPATCH ADDS A THIRD DISJUNCT TO THE 2026-09-09 LABEL CLAUSE, adjudicated 2026-09-25 on `#529` by the flagship's first-hand Q3 re-ruling, with GPT-6 Astra's amendments of 2026-09-26 (`crimewidthkill/rerule_20260925/`); this text is the codex seat's 2026-09-26 rewrite of the clause (`codexfarm_20260926/codex_carrierq3.txt`, "faithful with changes"), adopted with three corrections by the flagship's final ruling of 2026-09-30 (`crimewidthkill/final529r18_20260926/fable5_final.md`, item E): a dispatch LABEL is ALSO a floor axis when the edited guard's repair bypasses that dispatch in the enclosing function.  This is an alternative to selector data flow into the guarded body or re-testing after the join; absence of those two connections does not put a qualifying suppression label in clause (e). # *** SUPERSEDED.  DO NOT BUILD FROM THIS FILE OR FROM THE SPEC IT AMENDS. ***

**The eight amendments below were reconciled on 2026-09-17 and the answer was REWRITE, NOT
PATCH.**  The seat that wrote the original spec was asked to be harder on its own work than on
anyone else's, and ruled against it: *"Eight amendments on a spec that already disagreed with
itself (12 sessions vs 15 data rows) is a `#392` shape.  A builder applying them in order will get
1+6 wrong and will either treat `save 2s` as a duplicate of `kill 15` or REPLACE `kill 15` with
it, losing the `== 0x14` pin."*

> ## BUILD FROM `WITNESSSPEC_REWRITE_grok_20260917.txt` IN THIS DIRECTORY.
> It is standalone: 17 rows, 13 sessions, the CONTROL=DEAD set, the plant layouts, the tally
> (`14/17 -> 17/17`, **not** the `14/16 -> 16/16` the old spec claimed) and the clause-(e)
> disclosures.  No sticky notes.

**This file is kept for PROVENANCE ONLY** -- it records which amendments were right, which were
half-right, and which were aimed at the wrong document.  Reading it as instructions will build the
wrong probe.  Its conclusion is deleted rather than demoted, per the carrier's own rule that a
superseded rule must not keep the closing position of authority.

**Where each amendment landed:**

| # | subject | disposition in the rewrite |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | kill-row oracle: EOF ALONE => RED | **ADOPTED**, and it forces three further changes -- POST want becomes `m=13 sp once`, `DEST+4` is poisoned, and the tally names its reds |
| 2 | spec self-contradicts on session count | **ADOPTED and the number corrected again**: 13 sessions, not 12 and not 15 |
| 3 | the two RED rows may be duplicates | **HALF-RIGHT, and it misreads its own case.** The original two reds were `kill 14` and `kill 15` -- two LOAD holes catching `== 0x14` -- not Load vs Save. **Score `save 2s`, KEEP `kill 15`.** |
| 4 | accept rows check ARRIVAL not PRESERVATION | **ADOPTED as the canary, FOLDED into S7-S11 rather than new rows.** Its SVC32 complaint was against a generic heading; the `same svc` row was already right (SP unchanged IS the preservation check) |
| 5 | stimulus clears I\|F, so interrupts are on | **TRUE OF THE ENCODING, INERT FOR THIS PROBE** -- ARM IRQ dispatch is inside `if (!single_step)`, `fiq_asserted` has ZERO occurrences in `src/`, and the debugger `timer_stop()`s. # *** READ THIS BEFORE BUILDING `regress/arm_msrmode_probe.py`. ***
# The spec in `WITNESSSPEC_grok_20260917.txt` is AMENDED. Build the amended form, not the spec.

**WHY THIS FILE EXISTS, and it is not ceremony.** `#490` ran its pre-table check, prescribed two
rows at 16:05, and **lost both** when the row list was rewritten from a later panel's findings at
18:45. A measuring seat then built both mutants and both escaped the committed detector -- two
floor breaches the round's own artifact had predicted and specified. **Nothing was forgotten in the
sense of never known: the knowledge sat in the file that exists to be consulted at row-writing
time, and did not survive a rewrite in a DIFFERENT artifact.**

So the amendments live **beside the spec**, not in a ledger row, not in a checkpoint, not in a
panel transcript.

## AMENDMENT 1 -- *** THE KILL-ROW ORACLE IN THE SPEC IS BACKWARDS. DO NOT BUILD IT AS WRITTEN. ***

**Spec says:** an armed kill row is RED on pty EOF **AND** the exact text
`arm_load_register_bank: unimplemented mode 20`.

**BUILD INSTEAD:** on an armed kill row, **EOF ALONE => RED. The text is the DIAGNOSIS, not half
the trigger.** Record the text; do not gate on it.

**The mutant the AND cannot see** -- named by the glm seat, and it is the most plausible near-miss
fix anyone would actually write: **a build that still `exit(1)`s but with a REWORDED MESSAGE.**
The spec itself spotlights the `%i`-vs-`0x14` confusion, which makes *"fix the format string,
forget the `exit(1)`"* the obvious wrong repair. That mutant produces EOF and no matching text, so
the AND never fires, **the row goes GREEN, and the witness certifies a build in which a guest `MSR`
still kills the emulator.**

> **THE AND BUYS PROTECTION AGAINST A FALSE-RED AND PAYS WITH A FALSE-GREEN. For a CERTIFICATION
> witness that is the wrong trade, because the false-green is the direction that ships a defect.**

Under `EOF alone => RED` the feared cases still close correctly:

    crash elsewhere in the armed window  -> RED   conservative, and acceptable: any death in the
                                                  armed MSR's window satisfies the row's claim
    prints the line but does NOT exit    -> GREEN CORRECT.  The defect IS the process death.
                                                  A surviving process with a diagnostic is the fix.

**Buffering is not an objection** (checked, not assumed): `exit()` flushes stdio, stderr is
unbuffered, and a pty is line-buffered at the trailing newline -- so the defect's own death
preserves its own text. Buffering threatens only SIGNAL deaths, which is mutant territory.

*Single-seat finding, UNCORROBORATED: deepseek and minimax answered the same brief and neither
reached it. Recorded that way deliberately -- it is not a panel consensus. It is adopted because
the argument is sound, not because it was outvoted.*

---

## AMENDMENT 2 -- THE SPEC SELF-CONTRADICTS ON SESSION COUNT. RESOLVE BEFORE WRITING ROWS.

It states **"one fresh emulator process per data row"** and **"12 guest sessions"** for **15 data
rows**. `12 < 15`. Either three sessions host two rows each -- which reintroduces exactly the
sharing hazards the one-process rule exists to prevent -- or the count is wrong.

**Publish the row -> session schedule as a table in the probe header before writing any row.**
The spec's own stated exemptions (`enc ok` and the identity row have no session; `live` and
`pc adv` share one) account for some of the gap; make the arithmetic explicit rather than implied.

---

## AMENDMENT 3 -- THE TWO RED ROWS MAY BE DUPLICATES, WHICH WOULD HIDE HALF THE DEFECT

The spec expects `14/16 -> 16/16`, i.e. exactly TWO rows change colour, on the grounds that the fix
touches **two** `default:` arms (Load and Save).

**But pre-fix, the Save arm is not independently reachable** -- Load kills on ENTRY to the reserved
mode, so any Save-side row dies at its FIRST `MSR` with the identical EOF signature.

> **So the second red row differs from the first ONLY IF ITS STIMULUS CONTAINS A SECOND MODE
> WRITE.** If both are a single `MSR #0x14`, they are duplicates: **a Load-only fix turns both
> green and the witness certifies a build that still dies on the write OUT of the reserved mode.**

Write the second red row as `MSR #0x14` **then** `MSR #0x13` (the spec calls this shape `save 2s`
and disclosed it rather than scoring it). Its channel is
`arm_save_register_bank: unimplemented mode 20`.

---

## AMENDMENT 4 -- THE ACCEPT ROWS CHECK ARRIVAL AND NOT PRESERVATION

Two defects in the accept side:

* **SVC32 is incoherent as stated.** The spec's accept oracle is *"SP changed from boot value to
  0"*, but `MSR #0x13` from SVC32 hits the SAME-MODE SKIP -- save/load are never called -- so SP
  **cannot** change. That row's expectation must be *mode still `0x13`, SP UNCHANGED at `BOOT_SP`,
  and I|F cleared* (the c-byte write clears them), which the spec does say elsewhere. **Make the
  row's want match its own stimulus.**
* **A fix that repairs both LOAD arms and no-ops the SAVE arm passes every accept row**, because
  nothing checks that the outgoing bank was PRESERVED. Add a canary: write a known value to
  `r13`/`r14` before the switch and verify it is restored after returning, for every mode except
  USR32 (which is one-way).

---

## AMENDMENT 5 -- THE STIMULUS UNMASKS INTERRUPTS. KNOW IT BEFORE IT SURPRISES A ROW.

`MSR CPSR_c, #0x14` writes the WHOLE control byte, and bits 6-7 of `0x14` are **zero** -- so it
**clears I and F**, enabling IRQ and FIQ.

Pre-fix this is invisible (death is synchronous at the CPSR write). **Post-fix, the guest sits in a
reserved mode with interrupts enabled**, and any pending source takes an exception whose bank-save
runs with the reserved mode current -- so the Save arm can be entered ASYNCHRONOUSLY and row timing
becomes nondeterministic. Whether `testarm` has a pending source at that point is **not
established**; if a post-fix row flickers, this is the first thing to suspect.

---

## WHAT IS NOT AMENDED, AND WAS INDEPENDENTLY CONFIRMED

* **`0xE321F014` is correct.** Verified field-by-field against `to_be_translated` by the authoring
  seat AND hand-decoded independently by glm. `0x14` = **20** decimal, so `%i` prints `mode 20`.
* **`testarm` boots SVC32 (`0x13`) privileged, MMU off, T clear, SP `0x03fff000`** -- smoked on the
  committed binary, not inferred from the create path.
* **Ban `#0`** -- but NOT as a fake-green. `ARM_MODE_USR26` is `0x00` and there is no
  `case ARM_MODE_USR26` anywhere in `src/`, so `#0` reaches the same `default:` and KILLS. Ban it
  because it rows the **26-bit** hole, not the reserved 32-bit hole under test.
* *** THIS BULLET USED TO SAY "copy helpers from `arm_flags_probe.py`, NOT `arm_bfx_width_probe.py`,
  because BFX's `printed()` matches a bare `0x...` while the debugger prints `r0 = 0x...`".
  THAT REASON IS FALSE AND THE INSTRUCTION WOULD BREAK EVERY `print` ROW. ***
  `debugger_parse_expression` returns `PARSE_NUMBER` on success, so **`print <reg>` emits a BARE
  `0x...` line**; the `reg` command is the one that prints `r0 = 0x...`.  A builder who "fixed" the
  regex to expect `r0 = 0x` would score **every** `print` as ABSENT.
  **COPY `printed()` FROM `regress/lca_isalane_probe.py:398`** -- a 352-row instrument that works:
      `printed()` is the FIRST-match form: a `re.search` for the literal `print <reg>`,
      then optional whitespace, then a capture of `0x` + hex digits.  Read it from
      `regress/lca_isalane_probe.py:397-399` and COPY IT VERBATIM -- do not retype it, and
      do not reproduce it in this file, because the last attempt to quote it here had its
      backslashes eaten crossing a heredoc and landed as a regex split across two lines.
  and note `arm_flags_probe.py` **has no `printed()` at all**, so the original advice was not merely
  wrongly-reasoned, it was unfollowable.
  *Caught by the same seat that wrote the original instruction, on a later firing, and VERIFIED
  here against the probe before landing -- a wrong reason gets reused, and this one was sitting in
  the file whose title says READ BEFORE BUILDING.*
* **Never debugger-write `cpsr`.** That would skip Load and let the next guest `MSR` hit Save -- a
  state a guest cannot occupy on HEAD, i.e. laundering Save's unreachability.

## AMENDMENT 6 -- *** THE FAKE-GREEN MECHANISM NAMED IN THE SPEC (AND EARLIER IN THIS FILE) IS THE
## WRONG ONE.  GUARDING IT WOULD GUARD THE WRONG THING. ***

The spec, and my own earlier summary of it, say the hazard is: *if the CPU were already in USR32,
the mask fires, nothing switches, and THE KILL ROWS GO GREEN while the defect is live.*

**Measured against the row polarities, that is false.**  Already-USR32 gives `m=10`, alive, no Load
line -- and the row's want is the POST value `m=13`, so it goes **RED**.  That is a WRONG-REASON
FAIL (an `armed` / CONTROL failure), not a false green.  It still aborts the table, which is why
the arming prefix is right; but it is not the thing that could certify a live defect.

**THE REAL MSR FAKE-GREEN IS THE OPPOSITE SHAPE:**

    encoding is a nop  /  c-bit not set  /  the MSR is never stepped
        -> CPU stays SVC32, process alive, no Load line
        -> which is EXACTLY THE POST-FIX DECLINE APPEARANCE
        -> GREEN, on a build where the defect is live and simply was not exercised

> **A row that never fires its stimulus is indistinguishable from a row whose stimulus was
> correctly declined -- unless something proves the stimulus HAPPENED.**

**WHAT CATCHES IT, and all three are already in the spec -- they were just attributed to the wrong
hazard:** `enc ok` (verify imm + rotate + c-bit + SPSR-clear, NOT merely the MSR mask -- a mask-only
check blesses `#0x10`, which is USR32 and modelled), a `no cbit` row, and **the dump taken BEFORE
the MSR** so the arming word is on record independently of what the MSR then does.

*Same shape as the `#0` correction and the `printed()` correction: the ADVICE survives and the
REASON was wrong.  A wrong reason gets reused -- here it would have sent a builder to guard the
USR32 path, which reds honestly, while the path that actually greens went unguarded.*

---

## AMENDMENT 7 -- *** DO NOT COPY `arm_flags_probe.py`'s `session()` WRAPPER.  IT DUMPS ONCE, AT
## THE END, AND THIS PROBE'S PROCESS IS DEAD BY THEN. ***

`arm_flags_probe.py` takes its `dump` **after the whole program has run**.  That is fine for a probe
whose guest survives.

**This probe's guest calls `exit(1)`.**  The arming evidence -- `MRS r0,cpsr` stored at DEST -- must
be dumped **after `step 2` and BEFORE the killing `step`**.  A trailing dump cannot recover it,
because the process it would have read is gone.

    COPY from arm_flags_probe.py:   wait / send / rd, sw(), the -A colour trap, the stub load
    DO NOT COPY:                    its session() wrapper
    BUILD INSTEAD:                  an in-stream dump between the arming steps and the kill step

*This is also why the MSR probe is the right one to build FIRST: it is the only one of the three
that forces the mid-session dump, so building it proves that machinery before Thumb inherits it.
Thumb may use a trailing dump -- `-V` returns after `running = 0` and memory is still there.*

---

## AMENDMENT 8 -- A NOTE THAT BELONGS TO THE ALPHA SPEC, RECORDED HERE SO IT IS NOT LOST

`lca_isaphys`'s spec states its stop condition in PROSE (*"if `ptbr miss` / `ptbr sent` are red,
stop"*) and **does not emit `CONTROL=DEAD` / `RESULT=0/0`**.  Both ARM specs do.

**A failed `swpctx` would then yield a mid-score (~10/13) with the defect rows GREEN, and a
`RESULT=` grep will not refuse it.**  When that probe is built, copy the ARM token: emit
`LCAISAPHYS_CONTROL=DEAD` and `LCAISAPHYS_RESULT=0/0` and stop, rather than scoring a table whose
vehicle was never armed.

*The general rule, which is one idea rather than three checklists:* **a positive, un-fakeable
observable that the bad path is ARMED, checked on the SAME PROCESS that will issue the stimulus,
before any defect row is read -- and red there is SETUP-FAILED, not "no defect".**

## What the round does (rulings from pass 1 + the shared adjudication pass, both 8/8 seats + Opus 5.5; Fable on hold)
A reserved/unmodelled ARM mode (anything but 10 11 12 13 17 1b 1f) used to end the PROCESS at `arm_load_register_bank`'s /
`arm_save_register_bank`'s `default: exit(1)`.  Guest paths: `MSR CPSR_c,#0x14`; an SPSR poisoned by `MSR SPSR_c` then
restored by `MOVS pc,lr` (DPI S-to-PC) or `LDM {..pc}^` (arm_pop); and a NEVER-WRITTEN SPSR (cpu_new zero-fills: mode 0x00)
restored the same way -- no MSR needed.  The fix (member 3): decline the MODE FIELD only at those three writers (POLICY for an
UNPREDICTABLE result: DDI 0100I A2.5.7, glossary "must not halt or hang"; ARMv7 also permits UNDEFINED), latched per-CPU
announces with `(once)`, and both `default:` arms become latched backstops that return (reachable only by a debugger
`cpsr=` assignment).

371:     idx = (addr & 0xF) // 4
372:     val = None
373:     for line in t.splitlines():
374:         if line.startswith(label):
375:             parts = line[12:48].split()
376:             if len(parts) == 4 and len(parts[idx]) == 8:
377:                 val = int.from_bytes(bytes.fromhex(parts[idx]), "little")
378:     return val
379: 
380: 
381: def plant(ops, at=CODE):
382:     out = []
383:     for i, k in enumerate(ops):
384:         out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, WORDS[k]))
385:     out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * len(ops), WORDS["b_self"]))
386:     return out
387: 
388: 
389: def prologue():
390:     """POISON DEST..DEST+16 (the post-MSR stores must overwrite them) and point r4 at DEST."""
391:     return ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (DEST + o, POISON) for o in (0, 4, 8, 12, 16)] \
392:         + ["r4=0x%x" % DEST]
393: 
394: 
395: PREFIX = ["mrs_r3", "str_r3"]
396: 
397: 
398: def msr_cmds(ops, steps_after, late_end=DEST + 12, restore=False, regs=(), poke=(),
399:              chain=False):
400:     """Standard MSR session: prefix MRS/STR to DEST, arming dumps (DEST and the whole planted
401:     stream), MARK, the stimulus steps, then the result dump.
402:     #562: restore=True also plants TGT_OPS at TGT, sets lr=TGT and the LDMW word to TGT, and
403:     arms all three (a fall-through past the restore lands on b_self and reads `nofire').
404:     `regs' are debugger assignments made BEFORE the prefix; `poke' ones AFTER the arming dumps
405:     (a `cpsr=' poke must follow the prefix, whose MRS arms DEST == 0xd3).
406:     chain=True (with restore): TGT holds `movs pc,lr' and the readback TGT_OPS move to TGT2,
407:     where lr points -- so one session can run an LDM^ restore and then a MOVS restore."""
408:     full = PREFIX + ops
409:     cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
410:     extra = {}
411:     rb = TGT2 if chain else TGT
412:     if restore:
413:         cmds += plant(TGT_OPS, at=rb) + ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (LDMW, TGT),
414:                                          "lr=0x%x" % rb, "r5=0x%x" % LDMW]
415:         for i, k in enumerate(TGT_OPS + ["b_self"]):
416:             extra[rb + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
417:         extra[LDMW] = TGT
418:         if chain:
419:             cmds += plant(["movs_pc_lr"], at=TGT)
420:             extra[TGT] = WORDS["movs_pc_lr"]
421:             extra[TGT + 4] = WORDS["b_self"]
422:     cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
423:                           dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
424:     if restore:
425:         cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT, TGT + 4 * len(TGT_OPS)), dumpcmd(LDMW)]
426:         if chain:
427:             cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT2, TGT2 + 4 * len(TGT_OPS))]
428:     cmds += [MARK] + list(poke) + ["step %d" % s for s in steps_after] \
429:         + [dumpcmd(DEST, late_end)]
430:     return cmds, (full, extra)
431: 
432: 
433: def arming(early, full):
434:     """R4: DEST == 0xd3 after the prefix, and every planted word reads back as its WORDS entry.
435:     #562: `full' may be (ops, {addr: word}) -- the extra plants (restore target, LDM word)."""
436:     extra = {}
437:     if isinstance(full, tuple):
438:         full, extra = full
439:     if dumped(early, DEST) != BOOT_CPSR:
440:         return False
441:     for i, k in enumerate(full):
442:         if dumped(early, CODE + 4 * i) != WORDS[k]:
443:             return False
444:     for a, w in extra.items():
445:         if dumped(early, a) != w:
446:             return False
447:     return True
448: 
449: 
450: def prog_session(code_ops, blocks=(), data=None, regs=(), segments=((), ()), late_end=DEST + 12):
451:     """#562 pass 2: a multi-block program.  code_ops at CODE (after PREFIX), each (addr, ops) block
452:     at addr, each {addr: word} data word planted; every block and data word is ARMED (dumped and
453:     checked before MARK).  `segments' = ((pokes, steps), ...) after MARK: debugger assignments,
454:     then `step N'."""
455:     data = data or {}
456:     full = PREFIX + list(code_ops)
457:     cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
458:     extra = {}
459:     for a, ops in blocks:
460:         cmds += plant(ops, at=a)
461:         for i, k in enumerate(list(ops) + ["b_self"]):
462:             extra[a + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
463:     for a, w in data.items():
464:         cmds.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (a, w))
465:         extra[a] = w
466:     cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
467:                           dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
468:     for a, ops in blocks:
469:         cmds.append(dumpcmd(a, a + 4 * len(ops)))
470:     for a in data:
471:         cmds.append(dumpcmd(a))
472:     cmds.append(MARK)
473:     for pokes, steps in segments:
474:         cmds += list(pokes) + (["step %d" % steps] if steps else [])
475:     cmds.append(dumpcmd(DEST, late_end))
476:     early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
477:     return early, late, alive, marked, (full, extra)
478: 
479: 
480: def announces(t, key):
481:     """#562 pass 2: how MANY `(once)' lines the named site printed -- a latch that never
482:     suppresses prints twice when its site is visited twice; any() could not see that."""
483:     return sum(1 for ln in t.splitlines() if key in ln and "(once)" in ln)
484: 
485: 
486: def diag_unimpl(nm, t):
487:     """#562: which register-bank `unimplemented mode' text a session printed (HEAD: the
488:     function that exit(1)ed -- the reason under test; fixed: the backstop that announced)."""
489:     hits = sorted(set(re.findall(r"arm_(save|load)_register_bank: unimplemented mode (\d+)", t)))
490:     diagnosis.append("%s: %s" % (nm, ", ".join("%s mode %s" % h for h in hits) if hits
491:                                  else "no bank text"))
492: 
493: 
494: def announced(t, key):
495:     """#562: a `(once)' announce from the NAMED site -- the five sites share the token, so a
496:     bare `(once)' cannot tell a decline from a backstop, or Save's backstop from Load's."""
497:     return any(key in ln and "(once)" in ln for ln in t.splitlines())
498: 
499: 
500: def classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, cpsr_at=DEST + 4, sp_at=None, rt_at=None,
501:              once_key=None, pin_if=False, backstops=False, declines=False):
502:     """The got string.  EOF ALONE => dead.  Arming is checked first, from the EARLY half.
503:     #562: once_key names the announcing site; pin_if adds the CPSR I/F pair (field-only vs
504:     whole-operation decline); backstops adds ` save' / ` load' per Save/Load announce."""
505:     if not marked or not arming(early, full):
506:         return "unarmed"
507:     if not alive:
508:         return "dead"
509:     cpsr = dumped(late, cpsr_at)
510:     if cpsr is None or cpsr == POISON:
511:         return "nofire"
512:     got = "m=%02x" % (cpsr & 0x1F)
513:     if pin_if:
514:         got += " if=%d" % ((cpsr >> 6) & 3)
515:     if sp_at is not None:
516:         v = dumped(late, sp_at)
517:         #  #562 pass 2: ` sp0' is an ACTUAL zero readback (a never-loaded bank), not merely
518:         #  "not BOOT_SP" -- a missing or POISON slot now renders neither token.
519:         if v == BOOT_SP:
520:             got += " sp"
521:         elif v == 0:
522:             got += " sp0"
523:     t = early + late
524:     if (announced(t, once_key) if once_key else "(once)" in t):
525:         got += " once"
526:     if declines:
527:         for key, tag in (("msr_imm", "msr"), ("arm_pop", "pop"), ("dpi S-to-pc", "dpi")):
528:             if announced(t, key):
529:                 got += " " + tag
530:     if backstops:
531:         if announced(t, "arm_save_register_bank"):
532:             got += " save"
533:         if announced(t, "arm_load_register_bank"):
534:             got += " load"
535:     if rt_at is not None:
536:         v = dumped(late, rt_at)
537:         if v not in (None, POISON) and (v & 0x1F) == 0x13:
538:             got += " rt"
539:     return got
540: 
541: 
542: def report_dead(why):
543:     print("arm_msrmode_probe -- binary=%s label=%s machine=testarm" % (BIN, LABEL))
544:     print("ARMMSRMODE_CONTROL=DEAD")
545:     print("ARMMSRMODE_RESULT=0/0")
546:     print("  *** SETUP FAILED (%s) -- the vehicle is not the vehicle.  NOT 'no defect'. ***" % why)
547:     for nm, got, want, note in rows:
548:         print("  %-11s got=%-16s want=%-16s %s" % (nm, got, want, note[:56]))
549:     print("ARMMSRMODE_DONE")
550:     raise SystemExit(0)

556: #  the c-form from the f-form).

14:       line, where every post-MSR slot is still POISON -- so a correct fix would have read
15:       `nofire' on every kill row.  An `@MARK' pseudo-command becomes `print 0x5eed5eed' and
16:       the transcript is split at that token; arming is parsed only from before it, results
17:       only from after it.
18:   R3  `save 2s' plant and classifier agree, on the spec's own slots: return CPSR at DEST+8,
19:       SP at DEST+12, both inside the one dumped line.  The first draft planted SP at DEST+16
20:       and never dumped 0x9010, so ` sp' could never appear -- the row was ALWAYS red.  `rt'
21:       requires mode == 0x13 at the return slot, not mere existence.
22:   R4  the plant-dump of the MSR slot is SCORED: every MSR session dumps its whole planted
23:       stream before the stimulus and checks each word against WORDS, plus DEST == 0xd3 (the
24:       spec's "every later session repeats DEST==0xd3 + plant-dump").  A failure renders the
25:       row `unarmed', never `dead'.  This is what separates "died at the MSR" from "the MSR was
26:       never the instruction that ran".
27:   R5  CONTROL=DEAD stops the probe: `enc ok' red stops before any guest session; `live' or
28:       `pc adv' red stops before `armed'; `armed' red stops before any kill row.
29:   R6  `enc ok' note quoted the removed (w & 0x0FB0F000) mask; it now states the field-nibble
30:       test the code performs.  f_14 additionally checks rot == 0.
31:   R7  liveness: EOF on the pty is recorded; on EOF the child is reaped with a long deadline,
32:       without EOF with a short one (a live emulator never exits, and every live session used to
33:       burn the full 12 s).  The SIGKILL tail now READS THE STATUS: if waitpid shows the child
34:       exited on its own (not by our SIGKILL), alive is False.
35:   R8  canary accept rows also check arrival SP == 0 (spec: "arrive m=XX SP=0"), and `acc usr'
36:       now passes sp_at so a Load-skip (SP still BOOT_SP in USR32) reds it.
37: 
38: THE DEFECT, verified by reading cpu_arm.c at HEAD ef577140 -- PRE-#562; #562 replaced both exits
39: with latched backstops, so these lines describe the unfixed tree only:
40: 
41:     cpu_arm.c:606-608   arm_load_register_bank: default: fatal("...unimplemented mode %i"); exit(1);
42:     cpu_arm.c:559-561   arm_save_register_bank: default: same
43: 
44: `0xE321F014' is `MSR CPSR_c, #0x14' -- mode 0x14 is a RESERVED ARM mode, so the Load arm's
45: `default:' is reached and the process dies.  `%i' prints DECIMAL: `unimplemented mode 20'.
46: 
47: *** POLARITY: WANT IS POST-FIX CORRECT.  A PRE FAIL ON A DEFECT ROW *IS* THE REPRODUCTION. ***
48: *** ORACLE: EOF ALONE => DEAD.  THE TEXT IS DIAGNOSIS, NEVER HALF THE TRIGGER. ***
49: 
50: #562 THE FIX AND WHAT THIS PROBE NOW DETECTS.  Three guest writers can put an unmodelled mode
51: (anything but 10 11 12 13 17 1b 1f) into CPSR, and each now DECLINES THE MODE FIELD ONLY, with a
52: `(once)' announce naming its site: msr_imm (`MSR CPSR_c'), arm_pop's `LDM {..pc}^' SPSR restore,
53: and the DPI S-to-PC SPSR restore (`MOVS pc,..', `SUBS pc,lr,..').  The restores keep the CURRENT
54: mode in M[4:0] and assign every other bit from the SPSR (DPI later forces T for an odd destination
55: PC; not driven here).  The Save/Load `default:' arms are BACKSTOPS (latched announce, return, no
56: exit(1)), reached only once an unmodelled mode is ALREADY in CPSR, which no guest instruction can
57: now install -- only a debugger `cpsr=' assignment.  After such a plant Save's default: is reached
58: by a guest `MSR CPSR_c' to a modelled mode (dbg save), a DPI S-to-PC (dbg both, latch rpt) or
59: exception entry; Load's default: ONLY by a DPI S-to-PC executed in the planted mode (dbg both,
60: latch rpt).  An `LDM {..pc}^' from a planted mode stops the CPU at arm_pop's selector default:
61: first (pre-existing; not this fix).  POST-FIX the guest is in SVC32 with the I/F bits the write
62: set -- `#0x14' clears I and F -- NOT in a reserved mode.  Rows added by #562: kill 1e, kill 03,
63: rst movs, rst ldm, zero spsr, acc movs, acc ldm, dbg save, dbg both, latch ind, latch rpt, src
64: dpi/src pop x {fiq irq abt und svc}, msr lbl, encl, tbit, encl2, msr wide, the `if=' (I/F) pin on
65: kill 14 / kill 15, and ` sp0' (an actual zero SP readback) on the rows whose SP is a never-loaded
66: bank.
67: 
68: MODES 0x00-0x0F ARE NOW CLAIMED.  This header used to BAN `MSR CPSR_c, #0' as "the 26-bit hole,
69: a different defect".  The helper declines 0x00-0x0F like any other unmodelled value, and
70: `zero spsr' drives exactly mode 0x00 (an SPSR never written is zero: cpu_new zero-fills).
71: 
72: CLASS CLOSURE (repair 4) -- by the PER-BIT rule, on each path (msr_imm, DPI, arm_pop):
73:   (1) EVERY bit of the helper's operand is SET in at least one ACCEPT, so a widened projection
74:       declines a valid value somewhere:
75:         DPI / arm_pop: SPSR 0xffffffdf (src rows R1, R4: every bit but T) + 0x3f (tbit: T);
76:         msr_imm:       0xffffffd1 through `msr cpsr_fsxc,r0' (msr wide: bits 31:6, 4:0) +
77:                        `msr cpsr_c,#0x3f' (tbit: T).
78:   (2) At EVERY decline, every bit the decline RESTORES differs between the old CPSR and the new
79:       value, so a decline that keeps any bit from the old CPSR (or forces it) changes the
80:       readback:
81:         DPI / arm_pop: `msr cpsr_fsx,#0' + label re-entry before each decline leaves the old CPSR
82:                        0x0000000m (I F T clear) beside SPSR 0xffffffcf -- bits 31:6 differ at R2
83:                        (latch clear) and R3 (latch set); bit 5 (T) differs in tbit (SPSR 0x34 vs
84:                        the re-armed 0xd3);
85:         msr_imm:       the field-only decline writes the non-mode bits of the fields named:
86:                        msr wide declines 0x00000014 over 0xffffffd1 (bits 31:5 differ except T);
87:                        tbit declines 0x34 over 0xd3 (T, I, F differ); kill 14 (I/F).
88:   Mode bits: the restore keep-mode is checked per bit -- the declined SPSR sets mode bits 0-3
89:   and each has a label whose own bit is CLEAR on both paths (bit0 IRQ; bit1 FIQ; bit2 FIQ IRQ UND
90:   SVC; bit3 FIQ IRQ ABT SVC), so `~0x1e', `~0x1d', `~0x1b', `~0x17' and `~0x14' are all HELD
91:   (`~0x1b' measured killed 33/40 on both paths by Opus repair-3 table, and re-run here).  Bit 4 is
92:   EQUIVALENT (set in every modelled mode).  The msr_imm decline mask: bit 4 by kill 03 (SVC) and
93:   encl2 (`#0x03' from FIQ), bit 5 by tbit (`~0x3f').
94:   Labels x latch x outcome: each `src <path> <label>' session runs ACCEPT (latch clear), DECLINE
95:   (latch clear -> set), DECLINE (latch set), ACCEPT (latch set) in ONE process, one announce;
96:   {FIQ IRQ ABT UND SVC} x {clear, set} x {declined, accepted} x {DPI, POP} = 40 cells, all rowed.
97:   Enclosing predicates: DPI {rd==PC, rd!=PC} x {S, non-S} -- encl (non-S rd==PC `add pc,r6,#0';
98:   non-S rd!=PC `add r0,pc,#0') and encl2 (S rd!=PC `adds r0,pc,#0'; S rd==PC is every restore
99:   row); arm_pop {S, non-S} x {pc, no pc} -- encl (non-S with pc), encl2 (S without pc `ldmia
100:   r5,{r0}^'), the restore rows (S with pc).  DPI opcode: MOV (src, rst, ...) and SUB (encl2's
101:   `subs pc,lr,#0' declining 0x14).
102: 
103: CONTRACT (floor clause (e)) -- the edits this detector is DESIGNED to catch:
104:   * exit(1) reinstated at Save's default: (dbg save, dbg both, latch rpt) or Load's (dbg both,
105:     latch rpt);
106:   * the helper answering 1 always (kill 14/15/1e), admitting 0x1c-0x1e (kill 1e), dropping its
107:     `& ARM_FLAG_MODE' projection (src rows), or WIDENING it by ANY bit 31:5 (closure rule (1):
108:     src rows, tbit, msr wide, msr lbl);
109:   * the guard dropped at msr_imm (kill rows), arm_pop (rst ldm, src pop x FIQ IRQ ABT UND SVC) or
110:     DPI (rst movs, zero spsr, src dpi x FIQ IRQ ABT UND SVC), or restricted to ONE label, or to
111:     one unmodelled value such as 0x14 (src rows decline 0x0f), or compiled into the MOV instance
112:     only (encl2: `subs pc,lr');
113:   * the msr_imm guard skipped at one current-mode label (msr lbl: a decline from each of FIQ IRQ
114:     ABT UND SYS; latch rpt: SVC with the latch set);
115:   * the msr_imm decline clearing fewer than all five mode bits, at any label (`& ~0x0f': kill 03
116:     at SVC, encl2 at FIQ), or clearing MORE (`& ~0x3f', T lost: tbit); the restore keep-mode mask
117:     clearing fewer than bits 0-3 (src rows, per the bit/label table above);
118:   * a restore guard made unconditional, or unconditional for one label, or skipped / forced for one
119:     label x latch-state cell (src rows: all 40 cells; acc movs, acc ldm);
120:   * a WHOLE-OPERATION decline in msr_imm instead of field-only (the `if=' pin on kill rows; msr
121:     wide);
122:   * a decline taking ANY bit 31:5 from the old CPSR (or forcing it) instead of the SPSR / operand
123:     (closure rule (2): src rows R2/R3, tbit, msr wide) -- this replaces the repair-3 sentence
124:     "the declined SPSR has N Z I F SET while the CPSR it replaces has them CLEAR", which was FALSE
125:     for N and Z (the replaced CPSR then was c00000xx);
126:   * any two of the five latch sites sharing a bit (latch rpt: all five in one process; also
127:     dbg both, latch ind);
128:   * a latch that never SUPPRESSES (`|=' -> `&=', or the `|=' line deleted), or a repair applied
129:     only while its latch is clear (latch rpt: every site visited TWICE; src rows per label);
130:   * the enclosing predicates widened in ANY of their cells (see the closure paragraph): arm_pop's
131:     `if (return_flag)' (encl: non-S LDM with pc; encl2: S-LDM without pc), the DPI template's
132:     `rd == PC' compare (encl: non-S `add r0,pc'; encl2: S `adds r0,pc'), and the restore's
133:     `#ifdef A__S' (encl: non-S `add pc,r6,#0' -- NOT `mov pc,lr', which the decoder turns into
134:     `ret' and so never reaches the DPI template).
135: NOT CLAIMED, disclosed: unmodelled values a helper could get wrong unseen -- those never driven
136: through ANY path: 0x01, 0x02, 0x04-0x0e, 0x16, 0x18-0x1a, 0x1c, 0x1d.  Per path: msr_imm drives
137: 0x03 0x14 0x15 0x1e (and 0x14 with T set); DPI drives 0x00 (SVC only), 0x0f, 0x14 (SVC, and 0x34
138: in tbit); arm_pop drives 0x0f, 0x14 (SVC, and 0x34 in tbit).  ACCEPT-side provenance of the
139: restored bits: at R1/R4 the replaced CPSR and the SPSR agree on I/F, so a restore that kept I/F
140: from the old CPSR on an ACCEPT only would go unseen -- that is the pre-existing `cpsr = spsr'
141: assignment, outside the repair path (graded (e)), and any unconditional form is caught at R2.
142: DPI opcode instances other than MOV and SUB (graded (e): the opcode flows into nothing the repair
143: reads).  The msr_imm comment's "only the LOCAL mask changes: ic->arg[] is a cached translation"
144: -- no row re-executes ONE translated MSR, so a guard that wrote ic->arg[] would go unseen;
145: per-CPU latch isolation (one CPU only); exception entry as a Save-backstop route (no row);
146: T-bit / execution-state coherence on restore; the USR/SYS `LDM {pc}^' and `MSR SPSR' paths that
147: stop the CPU (filed separately).  The `tbit' row reads CPSR with the debugger's `print cpsr'
148: (low byte only: the raw word's top nibble can lag `flags') -- a rung-3 debugger observation.
149: 
150: NAMED-RED tally: HEAD = 17/43, RED exactly the 26 rows kill 14, kill 15, save 2s, kill 1e,
151: kill 03, rst movs, rst ldm, zero spsr, dbg save, dbg both, latch ind, latch rpt, the ten src *
152: rows, msr lbl, tbit, encl2 and msr wide, CONTROL=OK.  `encl' drives no unmodelled mode and is
153: GREEN on HEAD.  `save 2s' on HEAD is the SAME Load death as `kill 14' (EOF on the first MSR) -- it
154: is not an independent HEAD reproduction of Save; nor is `dbg both' a HEAD reproduction of Load
155: (HEAD dies in Save first).  43/43 on HEAD is a broken vehicle, not success.
156: 
157: Stay on `step', never `continue' (IRQ dispatch is inside `if (!single_step)').

174: 
175: CODE = 0x8000
176: DEST = 0x9000
177: BOOT_SP = 0x03FFF000
178: BOOT_CPSR = 0xD3
179: POISON = 0xDEADBEEF
180: MARK = "@MARK"
181: 
182: #  *** ONE WORDS DICT.  Every `put w' is drawn from here and every MSR session dumps its whole
183: #  planted stream against these entries before the stimulus (R4). ***
184: WORDS = {
185:     "mrs_r3":   0xE10F3000,              # mrs  r3, cpsr
186:     "str_r3":   0xE5843000,              # str  r3, [r4]
187:     "str_r3_4": 0xE5843004,              # str  r3, [r4,#4]
188:     "str_r3_8": 0xE5843008,              # str  r3, [r4,#8]
189:     "str_r3_c": 0xE584300C,              # str  r3, [r4,#12]
190:     "str_sp_8": 0xE584D008,              # str  r13,[r4,#8]
191:     "str_sp_c": 0xE584D00C,              # str  r13,[r4,#12]
192:     "str_sp_10": 0xE584D010,             # str  r13,[r4,#16]
193:     "mov_r0_2a": 0xE3A0002A,             # mov  r0, #0x2a
194:     "str_r0":   0xE5840000,              # str  r0, [r4]
195:     "b_self":   0xEAFFFFFE,              # b    .
196: }
197: for m in (0x03, 0x10, 0x11, 0x12, 0x13, 0x14, 0x15, 0x17, 0x1B, 0x1E, 0x1F):
198:     WORDS["c_%02x" % m] = 0xE321F000 | m     # msr cpsr_c, #m
199: WORDS["f_14"] = 0xE328F014                   # msr cpsr_f, #0x14
200: #  #562: SPSR poisoning and the two restore paths.
201: for m in (0x14, 0x1F):
202:     WORDS["s_%02x" % m] = 0xE361F000 | m     # msr spsr_c, #m
203: WORDS["movs_pc_lr"] = 0xE1B0F00E             # movs pc, lr       (DPI S-to-PC restore)
204: WORDS["ldm_r5_pc_s"] = 0xE8D58000            # ldmia r5, {pc}^   (arm_pop restore)
205: 
206: TGT = 0x8100                                 # restore target: lr, and the word at LDMW
207: LDMW = 0x9100                                # where `ldm_r5_pc_s' loads pc from

213: WORDS["add_lr_40"] = 0xE28EE040              # add  lr, lr, #0x40
214: WORDS["sf_c0"] = 0xE368F103                  # msr  spsr_f, #0xc0000000  (N and Z; 0x03 ror 2)
215: NZ = 0xC0000000
216: #  #562 repair 3: label entry WITH I and F set, the class-closure SPSR values, the chained-restore
217: #  operands, and the enclosing-predicate controls.

220: for m in (0xCF, 0xDF):

222: WORDS["movs_pc_r5"] = 0xE1B0F005             # movs pc, r5        (DPI S-to-PC; r5 is not banked)
223: WORDS["add_r5_40"] = 0xE2855040              # add  r5, r5, #0x40
224: WORDS["ldm_r6w_pc_s"] = 0xE8F68000           # ldmia r6!, {pc}^   (arm_pop restore, chained)
225: WORDS["ldm_r5_pc"] = 0xE8958000              # ldmia r5, {pc}     (NO ^: return_flag stays 0)

227: WORDS["add_r0_pc_0"] = 0xE28F0000            # add  r0, pc, #0    (Rn = PC, Rd != PC)

229: WORDS["str_r3_10"] = 0xE5843010              # str  r3, [r4,#16]

233: WORDS["msr_fsxc_r1"] = 0xE12FF001            # msr cpsr_fsxc, r1
234: WORDS["adds_r0_pc_0"] = 0xE29F0000           # adds r0, pc, #0
235: WORDS["ldm_r5_r0_s"] = 0xE8D50001            # ldmia r5, {r0}^
236: WORDS["subs_pc_lr_0"] = 0xE25EF000           # subs pc, lr, #0

238: WORDS["ss_ff"] = 0xE364F8FF                  # msr spsr_s, #0x00ff0000
239: WORDS["sx_ff"] = 0xE362FCFF                  # msr spsr_x, #0x0000ff00
240: WORDS["cf_0"] = 0xE32EF000                   # msr cpsr_fsx, #0
241: for m in (0x3F, 0x34):

243:     WORDS["s_%02x" % m] = 0xE361F000 | m

245: rows = []
246: diagnosis = []
247: 
248: 
249: def row(name, got, want, note=""):
250:     rows.append((name, str(got), str(want), note))
251: 
252: 
253: def session(cmds, settle=1.6, spin=1.6):
254:     """ONE FRESH PROCESS per scored row that executes a guest instruction.  Returns (t, alive);
255:     _session_split() turns the `@MARK' pseudo-command into the early/late split (R2)."""
256:     pid, fd = pty.fork()
257:     if pid == 0:
258:         os.execv(BIN, [BIN, "-V", "-A", "-E", "testarm", "-M", "64",
259:                        "0x%x:%s" % (CODE, STUB)])
260:         os._exit(127)
261:     buf = ""
262:     eof = False
263: 
264:     def rd(t):
265:         nonlocal buf, eof
266:         end = time.time() + t
267:         while time.time() < end:
268:             try:
269:                 r, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], 0.1)
270:             except OSError:
271:                 eof = True
272:                 return
273:             if not r:
274:                 continue
275:             try:
276:                 d = os.read(fd, 65536)
277:             except OSError:
278:                 eof = True
279:                 return
280:             if not d:
281:                 eof = True
282:                 return
283:             buf += d.decode("utf-8", "replace")
284: 
285:     rd(settle)
286:     for c in cmds:
287:         if eof:
288:             break
289:         try:
290:             os.write(fd, (c + "\n").encode())
291:         except OSError:
292:             eof = True
293:             break
294:         rd(0.3)
295:     rd(spin)
296: 
297:     #  *** POLL, NEVER SAMPLE ONCE. ***  rd() returns immediately on EOF, so a dying child must
298:     #  be given time to die.  On EOF reap with a long deadline; without EOF a short one (R7).
299:     alive = True
300:     deadline = time.time() + (12.0 if eof else 3.0)
301:     while time.time() < deadline:
302:         try:
303:             wpid, _st = os.waitpid(pid, os.WNOHANG)
304:         except ChildProcessError:
305:             alive = False
306:             break
307:         if wpid == pid:
308:             alive = False
309:             break
310:         time.sleep(0.05)
311:     if alive:
312:         try:
313:             os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
314:         except OSError:
315:             pass
316:         try:
317:             _w, st = os.waitpid(pid, 0)
318:             #  R7: if the child exited on its own between the last poll and our kill, the
319:             #  status says so -- do not leave alive=True for a process that died by itself.
320:             if os.WIFEXITED(st) or (os.WIFSIGNALED(st) and os.WTERMSIG(st) != signal.SIGKILL):
321:                 alive = False
322:         except ChildProcessError:
323:             pass
324:     try:
325:         os.close(fd)
326:     except OSError:
327:         pass
328:     return buf.replace("\r", ""), alive
329: 
330: 
331: def _session_split(cmds):
332:     #  Insert a unique, harmless marker command at MARK so the split survives \r stripping:
333:     #  `print 0x5eed5eed' is answered by the debugger (sh_intprio_probe.py measured `print
334:     #  0x1234'), and even if it were not, its echo carries the token.
335:     real = []
336:     for c in cmds:
337:         real.append("print 0x5eed5eed" if c == MARK else c)
338:     t, alive = session(real)
339:     i = t.find("5eed5eed")
340:     if i < 0:
341:         return t, "", alive, False
342:     #  late = everything after the marker's answer line (skip echo + answer)
343:     j = t.rfind("5eed5eed")
344:     nl = t.find("\n", j)
345:     late = t[nl + 1:] if nl >= 0 else ""
346:     return t[:i], late, alive, True
347: 
348: 
349: def printed(t, reg):
350:     #  COPIED VERBATIM from regress/lca_isalane_probe.py.  `print <reg>' emits a BARE `0x...'.
351:     m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
352:     return None if not m else int(m.group(1), 16)
353: 
354: 
355: def dumpcmd(addr, end=None):
356:     """*** ALWAYS DUMP FROM THE 16-BYTE-ALIGNED BASE. ***  debugger_cmd_dump does
357:     `addr = addr_start & ~0xf' and blank-pads groups before addr_start (arm_bdt_probe.py
358:     read_mem32(), measured 2026-09-11)."""
359:     base = addr & ~0xF
360:     top = ((end if end is not None else addr) + 16) & ~0xF

 succeeded in 281ms:
721:                         ("acc sys", "c_1f", 0x1F)):
722:     cmds, full = msr_cmds([key, "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8",
723:                            "c_13", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "str_sp_10"], [8],
724:                           late_end=DEST + 16)
725:     early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
726:     if not marked or not arming(early, full):
727:         got = "unarmed"
728:     elif not alive:
729:         got = "dead"
730:     else:
731:         arrive, sp_arr = dumped(late, DEST + 4), dumped(late, DEST + 8)
732:         back, sp_back = dumped(late, DEST + 12), dumped(late, DEST + 16)
733:         got = "%s/%s/%s/%s" % (
734:             "m=%02x" % (arrive & 0x1F) if arrive not in (None, POISON) else "nofire",
735:             "0" if sp_arr == 0 else "x",
736:             "m=%02x" % (back & 0x1F) if back not in (None, POISON) else "nofire",
737:             "sp" if sp_back == BOOT_SP else "nosp")
738:     row(nm, got, "m=%02x/0/m=13/sp" % want_m,
739:         "ACCEPT + CANARY in one row: arrive mode/SP==0 (banked, BSS-zero), return mode 0x13 with "
740:         "SP==BOOT_SP. A Save no-op returns SP=0; a Load no-op arrives with SP=BOOT_SP.")
741: 
742: cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_13", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [4])
743: early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
744: row("same svc", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8), "m=13 sp",
745:     "SAME-MODE: Load is not called, so SP-UNCHANGED *IS* the preservation check. Not 0xd3 -- "
746:     "#0x13 clears I|F.")
747: 
748: cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_10", "c_14", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4"], [4])
749: early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
750: row("usr mask", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full), "m=10",
751:     "In USR32 the c-byte is STRIPPED, so the following #0x14 must NOT reach the mode field. "
752:     "The host lives and the mode stays 0x10.")
753: 
754: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 rows
755: #  kill 1e: pins the helper's DOMAIN past the two driven reserved values -- a range form
756: #  admitting 0x1c-0x1e sends 0x1e to Load's backstop and reads m=1e.
757: cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_1e", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [1, 3])
758: early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
759: row("kill 1e", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, once_key="msr_imm",
760:                         pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
761:     "MSR CPSR_c, #0x1e: unmodelled, like 0x14/0x15. Kills a helper widened by RANGE to 0x1e.")
762: diag_unimpl("kill 1e", early + late)
763: 
764: #  kill 03: the only driven mode value with bit 4 CLEAR.  Every other MSR value here has bit 4
765: #  set, so a decline that cleared only M[3:0] from the mask (`& ~0x0f') still wrote bit 4 = 0 from
766: #  this value and reached Load's backstop with mode 0x03; the rows before this one could not see it.
767: cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_03", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [1, 3])
768: early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
769: row("kill 03", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, once_key="msr_imm",
770:                         pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
771:     "MSR CPSR_c, #0x03 (bit 4 clear): declined, mode unchanged. Pins the full 5-bit mode mask.")
772: diag_unimpl("kill 03", early + late)
773: 
774: #  The two SPSR restores.  `MSR SPSR_c, #0x14' poisons spsr_svc (I=F=0, mode 0x14); the restore
775: #  must land at TGT (else `nofire') in SVC32 -- current mode kept -- with I/F FROM THE SPSR
776: #  (if=0; a restore that skipped the CPSR write keeps the boot if=3) and the SVC SP.
777: for nm, op, site in (("rst movs", "movs_pc_lr", "dpi S-to-pc"),
778:                      ("rst ldm", "ldm_r5_pc_s", "arm_pop")):
779:     cmds, full = msr_cmds(["s_14", op], [6], restore=True)
780:     early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
781:     row(nm, classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, once_key=site,
782:                      pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
783:         "MSR SPSR_c,#0x14 then %s: the restore reaches Load's default: (HEAD dead). Post-fix: "
784:         "at TGT, mode kept, I/F from the SPSR, `%s' announce." % (op, site))
785:     diag_unimpl(nm, early + late)
786: 
787: #  zero spsr: NO MSR at all -- spsr_svc is never written on testarm (cpu_new zero-fills), so
788: #  `MOVS pc,lr' from boot SVC32 restores mode 0x00.
789: cmds, full = msr_cmds(["movs_pc_lr"], [4], restore=True)
790: early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
791: row("zero spsr", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8,
792:                           once_key="dpi S-to-pc", pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
793:     "MOVS pc,lr from boot SVC32 with spsr_svc never written (mode 0x00). HEAD dead.")
794: diag_unimpl("zero spsr", early + late)
795: 
796: #  The restores' ACCEPT side: a modelled SPSR mode must be APPLIED.  A guard made unconditional
797: #  (always keep the current mode) reads m=13 here.  SYS32 SP is the usr/sys bank's (0).
798: for nm, op in (("acc movs", "movs_pc_lr"), ("acc ldm", "ldm_r5_pc_s")):
799:     cmds, full = msr_cmds(["s_1f", op], [6], restore=True)
800:     early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
801:     row(nm, classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, pin_if=True),
802:         "m=1f if=0 sp0",
803:         "MSR SPSR_c,#0x1f then %s: SYS32 restored, SP from the usr/sys bank (0), no announce."
804:         % op)
805: 
806: #  The BACKSTOPS: reached only with an unmodelled mode already in CPSR, which only the debugger
807: #  `cpsr=' ASSIGNMENT can now install (no bank switch, no flags
808: #  sync).  NOT `put', which writes MEMORY.  The poke follows the arming dumps.  once_key names
809: #  a DECLINE site that must NOT announce: a plant is not a guest decline (dbg both: the DPI
810: #  guard runs only when an SPSR was copied, and mode 0x14 has none).
811: cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_13", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [4], poke=["cpsr=0xd4"])
812: early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
813: row("dbg save", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, backstops=True,
814:                          once_key="msr_imm"),
815:     "m=13 sp0 save",
816:     "cpsr=0xd4 then MSR CPSR_c,#0x13: Save's default: (HEAD exit(1)) announces and returns; "
817:     "Load(SVC) gives the never-saved svc SP: an actual 0, ` sp0'.")
818: diag_unimpl("dbg save", early + late)
819: 
820: cmds, full = msr_cmds(["movs_pc_lr"], [4], restore=True, poke=["cpsr=0xd4"])
821: early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
822: row("dbg both", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, backstops=True,
823:                          once_key="dpi S-to-pc"),
824:     "m=14 sp save load",
825:     "cpsr=0xd4 then MOVS pc,lr: no SPSR for 0x14, so BOTH Save's and Load's default: run in ONE "
826:     "instruction (Load's backstop and the distinct bits are ALSO carried by latch rpt).")
827: diag_unimpl("dbg both", early + late)
828: 
829: #  latch ind: ALL THREE guest declines in ONE process -- MSR CPSR_c,#0x14, then MSR SPSR_c,#0x14
830: #  and LDM {pc}^ (arm_pop) to TGT, whose MOVS pc,lr (DPI) restores the same poisoned spsr_svc to
831: #  TGT2.  Each site has its own mode_warned bit, so all three texts must appear; any two sharing a
832: #  bit print only the first.  (The other rows are each a fresh process and cannot see sharing.)
833: cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_14", "s_14", "ldm_r5_pc_s"], [8], restore=True, chain=True)
834: early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
835: #  once_key="register_bank": the generic ` once' token would be set by ANY of the three texts and
836: #  so carries nothing here; keyed on the backstops it pins their ABSENCE instead (no guest
837: #  decline may fall through to Save/Load).  The per-site ` msr pop dpi' tags are the channel.
838: row("latch ind", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, pin_if=True,
839:                           once_key="register_bank", declines=True), "m=13 if=0 sp msr pop dpi",
840:     "MSR, LDM^ and MOVS declines in ONE session: three distinct latch bits, three texts.")
841: diag_unimpl("latch ind", early + late)
842: 
843: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 pass 2: latch rpt
844: #  EVERY one of the five latch sites visited TWICE in ONE process.  Wants EXACTLY ONE announce per
845: #  site (a latch that never suppresses prints two; two sites sharing a bit print one between
846: #  them) AND the repair still applied on the second visit: the declines leave SVC32 (a=13/..),
847: #  the backstops leave the planted 0x14 alive (b=14/..).
848: #    CODE  c_14 c_15 (msr x2)  s_14  ldm r5 -> T1      T1  ldm r6 -> T2  (pop x2)
849: #    T2    movs pc,lr -> T3                            T3  add lr,#0x40 ; movs -> T4  (dpi x2)
850: #    T4    mrs ; str [r4,#4] ; str sp,[r4,#8]
851: #  then cpsr=0xd4, pc=T5, lr=T6:
852: #    T5    movs pc,lr -> T6  (save+load)   T6  add lr,#0x40 ; movs -> T7  (save+load again)
853: #    T7    mrs ; str [r4,#12] ; str sp,[r4,#16]
854: LR1, LR2, LR3, LR4, LR5, LR6, LR7 = 0x8100, 0x8140, 0x8180, 0x81C0, 0x8200, 0x8240, 0x8280
855: early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
856:     ["c_14", "c_15", "s_14", "ldm_r5_pc_s"],
857:     blocks=[(LR1, ["ldm_r6_pc_s"]), (LR2, ["movs_pc_lr"]), (LR3, ["add_lr_40", "movs_pc_lr"]),
858:             (LR4, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"]), (LR5, ["movs_pc_lr"]),
859:             (LR6, ["add_lr_40", "movs_pc_lr"]), (LR7, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "str_sp_10"])],
860:     data={LDMW: LR1, LDMW + 4: LR2},
861:     regs=["r5=0x%x" % LDMW, "r6=0x%x" % (LDMW + 4), "lr=0x%x" % LR3],
862:     segments=(((), 11), (["cpsr=0xd4", "pc=0x%x" % LR5, "lr=0x%x" % LR6], 6)),
863:     late_end=DEST + 16)
864: if not marked or not arming(early, full):
865:     got = "unarmed"
866: elif not alive:
867:     got = "dead"
868: else:
869:     def _half(ca, sa):
870:         c, v = dumped(late, ca), dumped(late, sa)
871:         if c in (None, POISON):
872:             return "nofire"
873:         return "%02x/%d/%s" % (c & 0x1F, (c >> 6) & 3,
874:                                "sp" if v == BOOT_SP else ("0" if v == 0 else "x"))
875:     t = early + late
876:     got = "a=%s b=%s msr%d pop%d dpi%d save%d load%d" % (
877:         _half(DEST + 4, DEST + 8), _half(DEST + 12, DEST + 16),
878:         announces(t, "msr_imm"), announces(t, "arm_pop"), announces(t, "dpi S-to-pc"),
879:         announces(t, "arm_save_register_bank"), announces(t, "arm_load_register_bank"))
880: row("latch rpt", got, "a=13/0/sp b=14/3/sp msr1 pop1 dpi1 save1 load1",
881:     "Five latch sites x2 in ONE process: exactly one announce each, repair kept on visit 2.")
882: diag_unimpl("latch rpt", early + late)
883: 
884: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 repair 3: src dpi / src pop
885: #  CLASS CLOSURE of label x latch x outcome x masked bits (see the header).  Per label and path,
886: #  ONE process, four chained restores, each read back:
887: #    enter the label with I|F set; SPSR := N|Z|0xdf; restore     -> ACCEPT, latch clear  (R1)
888: #    re-enter it with I|F CLEAR;  SPSR_c := 0xcf;   restore     -> DECLINE, latch clear (R2)
889: #                                                    restore     -> DECLINE, latch SET   (R3)
890: #                                 SPSR_c := 0xdf;   restore     -> ACCEPT, latch set    (R4)
891: #  DPI chains through `movs pc,r5' (lr is banked; r5 is not) with r5 += 0x40 per block; POP chains
892: #  through `ldmia r6!,{pc}^' over a table of block addresses.  Want R1 = R4 = c00000df and R2 = R3
893: #  = c00000c0|label (mode kept, N Z I F from the SPSR), and EXACTLY ONE announce from the site.
894: BLK = [TGT + 0x40 * i for i in range(4)]     # 0x8100 0x8140 0x8180 0x81c0
895: for lab, mode in (("fiq", 0x11), ("irq", 0x12), ("abt", 0x17), ("und", 0x1B), ("svc", 0x13)):
896:     ck = "c_%02x" % (0xC0 | mode)            # first entry: I|F SET (helper operand bits 7:6)
897:     cc = "c_%02x" % mode                     # re-entry: I|F CLEAR, so the declines' I/F (set in
898:                                              # the SPSR) can only have come from the SPSR
899:     for path in ("dpi", "pop"):
900:         if path == "dpi":
901:             early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
902:                 [ck, "sf_ff", "ss_ff", "sx_ff", "s_df", "movs_pc_r5"],
903:                 blocks=[(BLK[0], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "add_r5_40", "cf_0", cc, "s_cf",
904:                                   "movs_pc_r5"]),
905:                         (BLK[1], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_8", "add_r5_40", "cf_0", cc, "movs_pc_r5"]),
906:                         (BLK[2], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "add_r5_40", "s_df", "movs_pc_r5"]),
907:                         (BLK[3], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_10"])],
908:                 regs=["r5=0x%x" % BLK[0]],
909:                 segments=(((), 26),), late_end=DEST + 0x14)
910:             key = "dpi S-to-pc"
911:         else:
912:             early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
913:                 [ck, "sf_ff", "ss_ff", "sx_ff", "s_df", "ldm_r6w_pc_s"],
914:                 blocks=[(BLK[0], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "cf_0", cc, "s_cf", "ldm_r6w_pc_s"]),
915:                         (BLK[1], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_8", "cf_0", cc, "ldm_r6w_pc_s"]),
916:                         (BLK[2], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "s_df", "ldm_r6w_pc_s"]),
917:                         (BLK[3], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_10"])],
918:                 data={LDMW + 4 * i: BLK[i] for i in range(4)},
919:                 regs=["r6=0x%x" % LDMW],
920:                 segments=(((), 23),), late_end=DEST + 0x14)
921:             key = "arm_pop"
922:         if not marked or not arming(early, full):
923:             got = "unarmed"
924:         elif not alive:
925:             got = "dead"
926:         else:
927:             vals = [dumped(late, DEST + o) for o in (4, 8, 12, 16)]
928:             got = " ".join("nofire" if v in (None, POISON) else "%08x" % v for v in vals) \
929:                 + " n=%d" % announces(early + late, key)
930:         acc, dec = 0xFFFFFFDF, 0xFFFFFFC0 | mode
931:         row("src %s %s" % (path, lab), got, "%08x %08x %08x %08x n=1" % (acc, dec, dec, acc),
932:             "%s from %s: accept, decline, decline (latch set), accept; one announce."
933:             % (path.upper(), lab.upper()))
934:         diag_unimpl("src %s %s" % (path, lab), early + late)
935: 
936: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 repair 3: msr lbl
937: #  The msr_imm guard at every current-mode label it can run in (USR strips the c-byte): enter
938: #  FIQ, IRQ, ABT, UND, SYS in turn WITH I|F set, and in each write #0x14.  Each decline keeps the
939: #  label and clears I/F (the c-byte 0x14 has them clear).  One announce in total.
940: seq, blocks = [], []
941: for i, m in enumerate((0x11, 0x12, 0x17, 0x1B, 0x1F)):
942:     seq += ["c_%02x" % (0xC0 | m), "c_14", "mrs_r3", ("str_r3_4", "str_r3_8", "str_r3_c",
943:                                                       "str_r3_10", "str_r3_14")[i]]
944: early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(seq, segments=(((), 20),), late_end=DEST + 0x14)
945: if not marked or not arming(early, full):
946:     got = "unarmed"
947: elif not alive:
948:     got = "dead"
949: else:
950:     vals = [dumped(late, DEST + o) for o in (4, 8, 12, 16, 20)]
951:     got = " ".join("nofire" if v in (None, POISON) else "%02x" % v for v in vals) \
952:         + " n=%d" % announces(early + late, "msr_imm")
953: row("msr lbl", got, "11 12 17 1b 1f n=1",
954:     "MSR CPSR_c,#0x14 from FIQ IRQ ABT UND SYS: declined at every label, one announce.")
955: diag_unimpl("msr lbl", early + late)
956: 
957: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 repair 3: encl
958: #  The restores' ENCLOSING predicates.  SVC with spsr_svc = 0x1f (a VALID, different mode), then
959: #  three PC writes that must NOT restore it: a plain `ldmia r5,{pc}' (return_flag 0), a non-S
960: #  `add pc,r6,#0' (the A__PC template instance, not the `ret' shortcut that `mov pc,lr' decodes
961: #  to), and `add r0,pc,#0' (Rn = PC, Rd != PC: no PC write at all).  Wants m=13 after each and
962: #  r0 = its own address + 8.
963: E1, E2 = TGT, TGT + 0x40
964: early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
965:     ["s_1f", "ldm_r5_pc"],
966:     blocks=[(E1, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "add_pc_r6_0"]),
967:             (E2, ["add_r0_pc_0", "str_r0_c", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_8"])],
968:     data={LDMW: E1}, regs=["r5=0x%x" % LDMW, "r6=0x%x" % E2],
969:     segments=(((), 9),), late_end=DEST + 0x10)
970: if not marked or not arming(early, full):
971:     got = "unarmed"
972: elif not alive:
973:     got = "dead"
974: else:
975:     m1, m2, r0 = dumped(late, DEST + 4), dumped(late, DEST + 8), dumped(late, DEST + 12)
976:     got = " ".join("nofire" if v in (None, POISON) else f(v)
977:                    for v, f in ((m1, lambda x: "%02x" % (x & 0x1F)),
978:                                 (m2, lambda x: "%02x" % (x & 0x1F)), (r0, lambda x: "%x" % x)))
979: row("encl", got, "13 13 %x" % (E2 + 8),
980:     "Plain LDM {pc}, non-S ADD pc, ADD r0,pc with spsr_svc = 0x1f: none may restore the SPSR.")
981: 
982: T2, T3, T4, T5 = 0x8200, 0x8300, 0x8340, 0x8380
983: T6 = 0x83C0
984: early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
985:     ["s_3f", "ldm_r5_pc_s"],
986:     blocks=[(TGT, []), (T2, ["s_3f", "movs_pc_lr"]), (T3, ["c_3f"]), (T4, ["c_34"]),
987:             (T5, ["s_34", "ldm_r5_pc_s"]), (T6, ["s_34", "movs_pc_lr"])],
988:     data={LDMW: TGT}, regs=["r5=0x%x" % LDMW, "lr=0x%x" % TGT],
989:     segments=(((), 2), (["print cpsr", "cpsr=0xd3", "pc=0x%x" % T2, "lr=0x%x" % TGT], 2),
990:               (["print cpsr", "cpsr=0xd3", "pc=0x%x" % T3], 1),
991:               (["print cpsr", "cpsr=0xd3", "pc=0x%x" % T4], 1),
992:               (["print cpsr", "cpsr=0xd3", "pc=0x%x" % T5], 2),
993:               (["print cpsr", "cpsr=0xd3", "pc=0x%x" % T6, "lr=0x%x" % TGT], 2),
994:               (["print cpsr"], 0)))
995: if not marked or not arming(early, full):
996:     got = "unarmed"
997: elif not alive:
998:     got = "dead"
999: else:
1000:     vals = re.findall(r"print\s+cpsr\s*\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)", late)
1001:     t = early + late
1002:     got = " ".join("%02x" % (int(v, 16) & 0xff) for v in vals) + " n=%d" % (
1003:         announces(t, "msr_imm") + announces(t, "arm_pop") + announces(t, "dpi S-to-pc"))
1004: row("tbit", got, "3f 3f 3f 33 33 33 n=3",
1005:     "T-set accept via pop/DPI/msr (0x3f), T-set decline via msr/pop/DPI (0x34): T from the operand.")
1006: T7 = 0x8400
1007: early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
1008:     ["s_1f", "adds_r0_pc_0", "mov_r0_2a", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "ldm_r5_r0_s", "mrs_r3",
1009:      "str_r3_8", "s_14", "subs_pc_lr_0"],
1010:     blocks=[(T7, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "c_11", "c_03", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_10"])],
1011:     data={LDMW: 0x12345678}, regs=["r5=0x%x" % LDMW, "lr=0x%x" % T7],
1012:     segments=(((), 16),), late_end=DEST + 0x14)
1013: if not marked or not arming(early, full):
1014:     got = "unarmed"
1015: elif not alive:
1016:     got = "dead"
1017: else:
1018:     vals = [dumped(late, DEST + o) for o in (4, 8, 12, 16)]
1019:     got = " ".join("nofire" if v in (None, POISON) else "%02x" % (v & 0x1f) for v in vals)

331: def _session_split(cmds):
332:     #  Insert a unique, harmless marker command at MARK so the split survives \r stripping:
333:     #  `print 0x5eed5eed' is answered by the debugger (sh_intprio_probe.py measured `print
334:     #  0x1234'), and even if it were not, its echo carries the token.
335:     real = []
336:     for c in cmds:
337:         real.append("print 0x5eed5eed" if c == MARK else c)
338:     t, alive = session(real)
339:     i = t.find("5eed5eed")
340:     if i < 0:
341:         return t, "", alive, False
342:     #  late = everything after the marker's answer line (skip echo + answer)
343:     j = t.rfind("5eed5eed")
344:     nl = t.find("\n", j)
345:     late = t[nl + 1:] if nl >= 0 else ""
346:     return t[:i], late, alive, True

349: def printed(t, reg):
350:     #  COPIED VERBATIM from regress/lca_isalane_probe.py.  `print <reg>' emits a BARE `0x...'.
351:     m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
352:     return None if not m else int(m.group(1), 16)

354: 
355: def dumpcmd(addr, end=None):
356:     """*** ALWAYS DUMP FROM THE 16-BYTE-ALIGNED BASE. ***  debugger_cmd_dump does
357:     `addr = addr_start & ~0xf' and blank-pads groups before addr_start (arm_bdt_probe.py
358:     read_mem32(), measured 2026-09-11)."""
359:     base = addr & ~0xF
360:     top = ((end if end is not None else addr) + 16) & ~0xF
361:     if top <= base:
362:         top = base + 16
363:     return "dump 0x%x 0x%x" % (base, top)

365: 
366: def dumped(t, addr):
367:     """The word stored at `addr', from the LAST aligned dump line in `t' (R2: callers pass the
368:     early or the late half, never the whole transcript).  Memory-order bytes, little-endian
369:     guest: assemble like sw() in arm_flags_probe.py."""
370:     label = "0x%08x" % (addr & ~0xF)
371:     idx = (addr & 0xF) // 4
372:     val = None
373:     for line in t.splitlines():
374:         if line.startswith(label):
375:             parts = line[12:48].split()
376:             if len(parts) == 4 and len(parts[idx]) == 8:
377:                 val = int.from_bytes(bytes.fromhex(parts[idx]), "little")
378:     return val

381: def plant(ops, at=CODE):
382:     out = []
383:     for i, k in enumerate(ops):
384:         out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, WORDS[k]))
385:     out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * len(ops), WORDS["b_self"]))
386:     return out

388: 
389: def prologue():
390:     """POISON DEST..DEST+16 (the post-MSR stores must overwrite them) and point r4 at DEST."""
391:     return ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (DEST + o, POISON) for o in (0, 4, 8, 12, 16)] \
392:         + ["r4=0x%x" % DEST]

395: PREFIX = ["mrs_r3", "str_r3"]

397: 
398: def msr_cmds(ops, steps_after, late_end=DEST + 12, restore=False, regs=(), poke=(),
399:              chain=False):
400:     """Standard MSR session: prefix MRS/STR to DEST, arming dumps (DEST and the whole planted
401:     stream), MARK, the stimulus steps, then the result dump.
402:     #562: restore=True also plants TGT_OPS at TGT, sets lr=TGT and the LDMW word to TGT, and
403:     arms all three (a fall-through past the restore lands on b_self and reads `nofire').
404:     `regs' are debugger assignments made BEFORE the prefix; `poke' ones AFTER the arming dumps
405:     (a `cpsr=' poke must follow the prefix, whose MRS arms DEST == 0xd3).
406:     chain=True (with restore): TGT holds `movs pc,lr' and the readback TGT_OPS move to TGT2,
407:     where lr points -- so one session can run an LDM^ restore and then a MOVS restore."""
408:     full = PREFIX + ops
409:     cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
410:     extra = {}
411:     rb = TGT2 if chain else TGT
412:     if restore:
413:         cmds += plant(TGT_OPS, at=rb) + ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (LDMW, TGT),
414:                                          "lr=0x%x" % rb, "r5=0x%x" % LDMW]
415:         for i, k in enumerate(TGT_OPS + ["b_self"]):
416:             extra[rb + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
417:         extra[LDMW] = TGT
418:         if chain:
419:             cmds += plant(["movs_pc_lr"], at=TGT)
420:             extra[TGT] = WORDS["movs_pc_lr"]
421:             extra[TGT + 4] = WORDS["b_self"]
422:     cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
423:                           dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
424:     if restore:
425:         cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT, TGT + 4 * len(TGT_OPS)), dumpcmd(LDMW)]
426:         if chain:
427:             cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT2, TGT2 + 4 * len(TGT_OPS))]
428:     cmds += [MARK] + list(poke) + ["step %d" % s for s in steps_after] \
429:         + [dumpcmd(DEST, late_end)]
430:     return cmds, (full, extra)

432: 
433: def arming(early, full):
434:     """R4: DEST == 0xd3 after the prefix, and every planted word reads back as its WORDS entry.
435:     #562: `full' may be (ops, {addr: word}) -- the extra plants (restore target, LDM word)."""
436:     extra = {}
437:     if isinstance(full, tuple):
438:         full, extra = full
439:     if dumped(early, DEST) != BOOT_CPSR:
440:         return False
441:     for i, k in enumerate(full):
442:         if dumped(early, CODE + 4 * i) != WORDS[k]:
443:             return False
444:     for a, w in extra.items():
445:         if dumped(early, a) != w:
446:             return False
447:     return True

449: 
450: def prog_session(code_ops, blocks=(), data=None, regs=(), segments=((), ()), late_end=DEST + 12):
451:     """#562 pass 2: a multi-block program.  code_ops at CODE (after PREFIX), each (addr, ops) block
452:     at addr, each {addr: word} data word planted; every block and data word is ARMED (dumped and
453:     checked before MARK).  `segments' = ((pokes, steps), ...) after MARK: debugger assignments,
454:     then `step N'."""
455:     data = data or {}
456:     full = PREFIX + list(code_ops)
457:     cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
458:     extra = {}
459:     for a, ops in blocks:
460:         cmds += plant(ops, at=a)
461:         for i, k in enumerate(list(ops) + ["b_self"]):
462:             extra[a + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
463:     for a, w in data.items():
464:         cmds.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (a, w))
465:         extra[a] = w
466:     cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
467:                           dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
468:     for a, ops in blocks:
469:         cmds.append(dumpcmd(a, a + 4 * len(ops)))
470:     for a in data:
471:         cmds.append(dumpcmd(a))
472:     cmds.append(MARK)
473:     for pokes, steps in segments:
474:         cmds += list(pokes) + (["step %d" % steps] if steps else [])
475:     cmds.append(dumpcmd(DEST, late_end))
476:     early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
477:     return early, late, alive, marked, (full, extra)

480: def announces(t, key):
