482:     suppresses prints twice when its site is visited twice; any() could not see that."""
483:     return sum(1 for ln in t.splitlines() if key in ln and "(once)" in ln)

494: def announced(t, key):
495:     """#562: a `(once)' announce from the NAMED site -- the five sites share the token, so a
496:     bare `(once)' cannot tell a decline from a backstop, or Save's backstop from Load's."""
497:     return any(key in ln and "(once)" in ln for ln in t.splitlines())

500: def classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, cpsr_at=DEST + 4, sp_at=None, rt_at=None,
501:              once_key=None, pin_if=False, backstops=False, declines=False):
502:     """The got string.  EOF ALONE => dead.  Arming is checked first, from the EARLY half.
503:     #562: once_key names the announcing site; pin_if adds the CPSR I/F pair (field-only vs
504:     whole-operation decline); backstops adds ` save' / ` load' per Save/Load announce."""
505:     if not marked or not arming(early, full):
506:         return "unarmed"
507:     if not alive:
508:         return "dead"
509:     cpsr = dumped(late, cpsr_at)
510:     if cpsr is None or cpsr == POISON:
511:         return "nofire"
512:     got = "m=%02x" % (cpsr & 0x1F)
513:     if pin_if:
514:         got += " if=%d" % ((cpsr >> 6) & 3)
515:     if sp_at is not None:
516:         v = dumped(late, sp_at)
517:         #  #562 pass 2: ` sp0' is an ACTUAL zero readback (a never-loaded bank), not merely
518:         #  "not BOOT_SP" -- a missing or POISON slot now renders neither token.
519:         if v == BOOT_SP:
520:             got += " sp"
521:         elif v == 0:
522:             got += " sp0"
523:     t = early + late
524:     if (announced(t, once_key) if once_key else "(once)" in t):
525:         got += " once"
526:     if declines:
527:         for key, tag in (("msr_imm", "msr"), ("arm_pop", "pop"), ("dpi S-to-pc", "dpi")):
528:             if announced(t, key):
529:                 got += " " + tag
530:     if backstops:
531:         if announced(t, "arm_save_register_bank"):
532:             got += " save"
533:         if announced(t, "arm_load_register_bank"):
534:             got += " load"
535:     if rt_at is not None:
536:         v = dumped(late, rt_at)
537:         if v not in (None, POISON) and (v & 0x1F) == 0x13:
538:             got += " rt"
539:     return got

553: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- enc ok  (no guest)
554: #  MSR immediate:  cond 00110 R 10 field(19:16) 1111 rot(11:8) imm8.  The field nibble is
555: #  bits 19:16 and a mask cannot substitute for it (0x0FB0F000 kills bit 0x08 and cannot tell
556: #  the c-form from the f-form).

627:                 "str_r3_14": (0xE, 2, 0xC, 0, 4, 3, 0x14)}.get(k)
628:         if k == "ldm_r6w_pc_s":
629:             ok = (w >> 25) & 7 == 4 and (w >> 20) & 1 and (w >> 22) & 1 and (w >> 21) & 1 \
630:                 and (w >> 23) & 1 and not (w >> 24) & 1 and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 6 \
631:                 and w & 0xFFFF == 0x8000
632:         elif k == "ldm_r5_pc":
633:             ok = (w >> 25) & 7 == 4 and (w >> 20) & 1 and not (w >> 22) & 1 \
634:                 and not (w >> 21) & 1 and (w >> 23) & 1 and not (w >> 24) & 1 \
635:                 and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 5 and w & 0xFFFF == 0x8000
636:         else:
637:             ok = f == want
638:         if not ok:
639:             bad.append(k)
640:     if k == "ldm_r5_pc_s":
641:         #  LDM: bits 27:25 = 100, L set, S (bit 22) set, W clear, Rn = 5, list = {pc} only
642:         if (w >> 25) & 7 != 0b100 or not (w >> 20) & 1 or not (w >> 22) & 1 \
643:                 or (w >> 21) & 1 or (w >> 16) & 0xF != 5 or w & 0xFFFF != 0x8000:
644:             bad.append(k)

646:     "CONTROL=DEAD. Decoder match on the PLANTED literals: MSR-imm frame (w & 0x0FB0F000) == "
647:     "0x0320F000, field nibble (w>>16)&0xF = 0001 c-form / 1000 f-form, rot 0, bit 22 clear, imm8 "
648:     "== the key's mode.")

codex
I found two coverage gaps in the stated contract. The full-word restore declines check upper bits only when they are set, so forcing one of those bits to 1 could escape. The MSR mask claim also says “at any label,” but only SVC and FIQ receive a decline that distinguishes mode bit 4. These are static findings; I have not run mutants.

       MSR   bits 31:6,4:0 msr wide (0xffffffd1, NEW ROW); bit 5 tbit (c_3f);
             bits 3:1 of the MODE are set in accepts by c_17/c_1b/c_1f (msr lbl, acc rows)  -- complete
   (2) every restored bit differs old-vs-new at each decline:
       DPI   31:6 at R2 and R3 (src); 5 at tbit (0x34 over re-armed 0xd3)       -- complete
       POP   same                                                               -- complete
       MSR   c-byte I/F at kill 14 etc.; T at tbit; 31:8 + I/F at msr wide
             (0x00000014 over 0xffffffd1: every non-mode bit except T differs)  -- complete
   Missing cells: NONE against the rule.  Accept-side provenance (R1/R4 I/F equal in both
   sources) is OUTSIDE the rule and disclosed as (e).

2. (e) ITEMS -- graded
   DPI repair compiled into MOV only        -> ROWED (encl2 subs pc,lr)
   msr `~0x0f' at non-SVC labels            -> ROWED (encl2 #0x03 from FIQ)
   `mask &= ~0x3f'                          -> ROWED (tbit)
   accept-side I/F provenance               -> DISCLOSED (pre-existing assignment; unconditional
                                               forms caught at R2)
   msr operand bits 31:8                    -> ROWED (msr wide, new)

3. RECORDS corrected in the probe header (all six Opus listed):
   (a) WIDENING: now "by ANY bit 31:5" with the per-bit carriers named.
   (b) N/Z provenance: the false "replaced CPSR has them CLEAR" sentence replaced by rule (2),
       and quoted as the corrected wording.
   (c) rd==PC: both S and non-S cells named (encl / encl2).
   (d) msr mask width: kill 03 (SVC) + encl2 (FIQ) for bit 4; tbit for ~0x3f.
   (e) "bits 7:5 SET" (PRETABLE3 and header) replaced by the per-bit carrier list.
   (f) `~0x1b': stated HELD (killed on both paths), not disclosed.
   PRETABLE3's "no BUILT narrowing is UND-visible ... ~0x1b, not built" is superseded by this.

4. PER ROW -- kills: <mutant> via <channel>   (rows new or changed in repairs 3-4)
   src dpi <lab>  R_dpi_clr17/1e/1d/1b per bit/label table, H_proj_ff, H_proj_c (R1/R4 declined; H_proj_3f is NOT
                  visible here -- 0xffffffdf has T clear -- tbit carries it),
                  N_dpi_keepflags/keepN/keepNZ/grok, F_dpi_iclr (R2/R3 word), L_dpi_latchlabel,
                  E_dpi_irqlatch/irqacc, N_dpi_svc_only, N_dpi_keep_irq, M_drop_dpi,
                  M_uncond_dpi, N_latch_and_dpi, N_cond_dpi, D_del_dpi (n=), H_proj_11f (R1)

   tbit           H_proj_3f (accepts of 0x3f declined -> 33), M_msr_clr3f (msr decline loses T)
   encl           C_pop_retflag1, D_dpi_pccmp1, D_dpi_snots
   encl2          D_dpi_pccmpS (adds r0,pc jumps), C_pop_retflagS (ldm {r0}^ restores),

   msr lbl        E_msr_notfiq, N_msr_clr1e, H_proj_ff (c_dX entries)
   latch rpt      every latch-suppression / alias / cond mutant, X_msr_latchsvc
   kill rows, rst/acc/dbg/latch ind: as PRETABLE2.
   CHANNEL: CPSR words, r0 value, exact announce counts, `print cpsr' low byte, alive/dead --
   none survive-only.

5. THE TABLE: 91 mutants = mutr.py 10 + mutr2.py 13 + mutate2.py 36 + mutate3.py 6 (the 65) +
   Opus mutr3b.py 24 (its 18 new + 6 re-runs, a superset of mutr3.py) + mutate5.py 2 (rule
   probes).  4 lanes, own clone each, BASE before/after per lane.
   Predicted equivalent: NONE (Opus graded no built mutant equivalent; its only equivalence is

END PRETABLE4
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""mutate5.py <tree> <mutant>|LIST -- #562 repair 4 additions: two probes of closure rules (1)/(2)
at cells the closure claims but no earlier mutant targeted.  Presence-pinned (count == 1)."""
import io
import sys
T, M = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
C = "src/cpus/cpu_arm.c"
I = "src/cpus/cpu_arm_instr.c"
MUT = {
    # rule (2) on the msr path: the field-only decline keeps NZCV from the old CPSR
    "W_msr_keepflags": [(I, "\t\tmask &= ~ARM_FLAG_MODE;\n", "\t\tmask &= ~(ARM_FLAG_MODE | 0xf0000000);\n")],
    # rule (1) at a bit no earlier mutant widened by: bit 8 (A)
    "H_proj_11f": [(C, "\tswitch (mode & ARM_FLAG_MODE) {", "\tswitch (mode & 0x11f) {")],
}
if M == "LIST":
    print(" ".join(MUT))
    sys.exit(0)
for f, a, b in MUT[M]:
    p = T + "/" + f
    s = io.open(p, newline="").read()
    n = s.count(a)
    if n != 1:
        print("MUTANT-TEXT-NOT-FOUND %s %s count=%d" % (M, f, n))
        sys.exit(3)
    io.open(p, "w", newline="").write(s.replace(a, b))
print("MUTANT-APPLIED %s" % M)

520:  *  arm_mode_is_modelled():
521:  *
522:  *  #562: 1 if M[4:0] of `mode' is one of the seven modes this model banks
523:  *  (10 11 12 13 17 1b 1f), else 0.  Its case list MUST stay equal to the
524:  *  case lists of arm_save_register_bank() and arm_load_register_bank()
525:  *  below: the three switches together define "modelled", and those two
526:  *  default: arms are reached only for a mode this returns 0 for.  Extern,
527:  *  not static: the DPI template is compiled as its own object.
528:  */
529: int arm_mode_is_modelled(uint32_t mode)
530: {
531: 	switch (mode & ARM_FLAG_MODE) {
532: 	case ARM_MODE_USR32:
533: 	case ARM_MODE_SYS32:
534: 	case ARM_MODE_FIQ32:
535: 	case ARM_MODE_IRQ32:
536: 	case ARM_MODE_SVC32:
537: 	case ARM_MODE_ABT32:
538: 	case ARM_MODE_UND32:

540: 	default:return 0;
541: 	}

4834: 		 *  The optimized functions do not support show_trace_tree,

4839: 		 *  byte-order term), so on this LE host a BE guest's LDM/STM
4840: 		 *  through them is byte-reversed per word, and 23 of the 256
4841: 		 *  handlers load PC, i.e. a reversed RETURN ADDRESS. bdt_load/

4844: 		 *  every BE multi-transfer correct at the cost of the fast path
4845: 		 *  no BE guest today can boot to use. Resolving the guest term
4846: 		 *  at translate time is sound because cpu->byte_order cannot

VERDICT: DO-NOT-SHIP | 3 floor breaches

The breaches concern the shipped detector and its contract. I found no new defect in the current product implementation. Everything below is static review; I wrote nothing and ran no builds, detectors, or gates.

**1 | Yes: a small fix-side edit is predicted to preserve 43/43.**

- **Forced-bit coverage:** adding `| ARM_FLAG_N` to the DPI decline merge at [cpu_arm_instr_dpi.c:247](C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/cpus/cpu_arm_instr_dpi.c:247) would force N set. Every fully scored DPI decline already expects N set; the other decline observations check mode/I/F or the low byte. Thus **43/43 survival is predicted, not measured**. The same observation gap covers **24 upper bits × 2 restore paths = 48 force-one cases**. Old/new disagreement detects retaining the old value; it does not detect forcing the expected constant.

- **MSR mask coverage “at any label”:** a bit-4 omission confined to IRQ is indistinguishable in these rows. IRQ declines only `0x14`, whose bit 4 already matches IRQ’s. SVC/FIQ’s `0x03` observations do not cover that cell. Across **6 labels × 5 mode bits**, differing operands cover **20/30 cells**: SVC 5, FIQ 4, IRQ 2, ABT 2, UND 4, SYS 3. This contradicts [the contract](C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/arm_msrmode_probe.py:115).

- **POP enclosing predicate:** only **3/4** `{S, non-S} × {PC, no PC}` cells are driven. **Non-S/no-PC is absent**, despite the “ANY … cells” promise. It is reachable through [bdt_load’s call to arm_pop](C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/cpus/cpu_arm_instr.c:1992). A widening confined to that fourth cell has no stimulus.

The remaining closure denominator checks out **in aggregate**: **96/96 accept bit/path cells**, **81/81 non-mode old/new-difference bit/path cells**, and the **40 restore label × latch × outcome cells**. Constant forcing requires checking both expected polarities—**162 bit/path/polarity cells**—which the old/new inequality rule does not establish.

No accept operand bit is missing across an entire path. But the literal **“at EVERY decline”** premise is false: T is zero in both sources at `src` R2/R3 and `msr wide`; `tbit` supplies its disagreement separately.

The isolated measurements worth requesting are: DPI decline forcing N set, IRQ-specific MSR bit-4 omission, and POP predicate widening confined to non-S/no-PC.

**2 | Yes, records still need correction.**

Besides the three contract overclaims above:

- [Header line 76](C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/arm_msrmode_probe.py:76) and PRETABLE4 incorrectly credit `0xffffffd1` with all bits 4:0; bits 3:1 are clear. Other accepts supply them.
- “At EVERY decline” must describe a **union across observations**, not every individual decline.
- The ACCEPT-only I/F disclosure needs qualification: a global accept-only retention error is caught by `acc movs`/`acc ldm`, which assert I/F clear after boot I/F set. Particular `src` cells still lack that distinction.
- [Inline source-row comments](C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/arm_msrmode_probe.py:887) still describe `c00000df`/`c00000c0|mode`, rather than the actual `ffffffdf`/`ffffffc0|mode`.

The measured tally is consistent: fixed **43/43**, HEAD **17/43**, exactly **26 named reds**. The mutation denominator is **91 executions / 85 distinct mutant names**, including six disclosed reruns.

**3 | Yes.**

The untested invalid modes and repeated translated-MSR behavior are guest-reachable parts of the repair’s semantics. Of **25 unmodelled mode values**, **6** are driven anywhere and **19** are not; per-path coverage is MSR **4/25**, DPI **3/25**, POP **2/25**. Disclosure identifies those limits; it does not establish coverage.

The current helper’s seven cases match both bank switches, and the backstop reachability comments hold. The additional finding beyond the two bit-coverage gaps is the missing fourth POP predicate cell.
